Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The numerical_analysis operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 825. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 586 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 825 + O(1/n^586). Summing over all modes and applying the numerical_analysis convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The numerical_analysis operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 955. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 900 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

For the inductive step, we decompose the numerical_analysis structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 900. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
